# ROADMAP — open work

Open work only, one line per item: `id — symptom (headline cell); code site; fix shape; oracles; STATUS`. Grounding re-measures every claim (LOOPROMPT §1), so a line keeps only what a re-measurement cannot recover: the id, the code site, a held design's branch or commit, a named prerequisite.
A finished slice's record is its commit message, anchored `§4.5.N` (bug, mechanism, byte-identity, review): find it with `git log --grep '§4.5.N'`; entries up to §4.5.582 are in `git show acabe991:docs/history/ROADMAP_ARCHIVE.md`. Done = delete the closed line(s), add each new find as one line in its section, recount the Summary.
Section numbers and row ids are stable, never renumbered or reused; source comments cite them (`ROADMAP §2 🆕 I ⓐ`, `§3 ⑭`, `§3.b <slug>`, `§5.1-be`). No gate or test counts in any doc: CI on 3 OSes is the record, and `format_version` is `crates/vita-artifact/src/header.rs:15`.
STATUS: OPEN = startable (two oracles, or one plus hand-IEEE, and no unmet prerequisite). Nothing else starts: BLOCKED (a named prerequisite, listed in [REMAINING_WORK.md](REMAINING_WORK.md) §D), WALL, SPLIT (oracle split, never chased), HELD (deliberate or trigger-gated), RECORD (observation), LOUD, PERF, DEFERRED, DO-NOT-START, DUP.

## 0. correct-support promotion queue

Deliberately loud, not gaps: `new[]` on a fixed array; a multi-dim partial index `s[0]`; a cross-type SoA whole-element copy; a `real` scrutinee in a `generate case`.

### iverilog defects (vita is IEEE-correct) — oracle disqualifiers, regression-pinned

- ① `.len()` of a `string s[5]` element holding "abcdefg" — iverilog 5, vita 7
- ② concurrent fork activations share an `automatic` string array — iverilog `A!`, vita `A!!`
- ③ `$fmonitor` twice on one fd — iverilog accumulates, vita replaces per destination
- ④ `%s` of an empty string-array element — iverilog a blank, vita empty
- ⑤ `$clog2(4'sd7+4'sd1)` — iverilog 32, vita 3 (= verilator, §20.8.1)
- ⑥ `$itor(64'h1_0000_0008)` — iverilog 8, vita 4294967304 (= verilator)
- ⑦ `s<"ab"`, `s<"aa"`, `s<"zz"` with `s="ab"` — iverilog all 1, vita `0 0 1` (= verilator)
- ⑧ an `automatic time signed` frame local — iverilog aborts, vita `-4` (verilator alone)
- ⑨ a continuous assign's function at t0 — iverilog calls `f2(a | 1'b0)` and `f3(av[0])` on x first (`f2 t=0 x=x`, `WARNING: …: value is unhandled for priority or unique case statement` `Time: 0`), but `f1(a)` only on the written value (`f1 t=0 x=0`); vita calls all three once on the value (= verilator; `t0_call_cont_assign_hold.rs` `an_expression_argument_is_not_called_on_x`)
- ⑩ a constant -0.0 (`-0.0`, `-(0.0)`, `-5.0*0.0`, `0.0 * -1`, `parameter real P = -0.0`, `$ceil(-0.3)`) and a runtime `-z` of +0.0 — iverilog stores +0.0 (`$realtobits` 0, `%f` `0.000000`) yet folds `1.0/(-0.0)` to -inf and `$atan2(-0.0, -1.0)` to −π; vita stores -0.0 and prints `-0.000000` (= verilator, IEEE 754 negation; `real_signed_zero_render.rs` `z03_constant_negative_zero_follows_its_bits`)

- 14-b — `-P<path>=<val>` unimplemented; BLOCKED (§8 `defparam` non-goal)

## 0-B. Small follow-ons (kept loud)

- `void'(getnext())` with an output formal; a frame-formal array forwarded into a nested hierarchical call; `8'(P*a)` over a param or call leaf.
- fork-in-frame: `fork_arms_self_contained`'s duplicate re-walk; an elaborate reject for a fork arm calling a forking task (F4004 guards it); zero-delay sibling visibility not differentially verified.

## 0-C. Large items — start decision table (do not re-estimate the size)

- A. file-position family (`$ftell`, `$fseek`, `$rewind`, `$ferror`) — a new `SysFuncId` = frozen-root bump, no sidecar route; payoff medium; `$feof` / `$fgetc` / `$ungetc` alone need no bump.
- B. shared literal-parsing crate — medium-large; payoff small (one two-predicate hazard); trap: `literal.rs` needs `sim_ir` (layering inversion), so split digit→bits from `ConstVal` packing.
- Order A > B.

## 1. Start priority — principle only (the live queue is §5.2)

① CRITICAL silent-wrong with an oracle (§2) > ② loud→supported with an oracle (§3) > ③ honest-loud promotion whose prerequisite holds (§0, §4, §5) > ④ G2 OBS (§6).
Performance is off the ladder. No oracle is not a reason to defer: build from the LRM, pin by hand. Inside a band a corpus witness goes first (`ibex` leads); a §2 row without one is frozen. Do not create a new queue here. T4: a function-local array element write is ~20× iverilog's cost.

## 2. Silent-wrong residues

Row ids are cited from source (`§2 row 7`, `§2 🆕 I`); a number is never reused.

FROZEN (owner direction): synthetic-probe rows stay out of §5.2. A row re-enters when a workload-corpus design hits it, when it lies in a corpus row's fix path, or when an external report reproduces it, and is then re-measured in full. Pre-existing finds outside a slice's fix path go to [PROBE_CATALOG.md](PROBE_CATALOG.md).

WALL(AST self-width): a tree-wide pass giving a node's self width without lowering it (today only inside a cast: `const_self_width` + `const_signed_env`).

(b) waves are ordered per net, so links sharing one vector or array net run as a cycle (d18, six generate instances through `o[k]`: 18 calls, both oracles 6; e02 `c[k+1] = f(c[k])`: 9, iverilog 10, verilator 13); (c) a held cycle is released whole with everything below it, so a downstream link runs on a held net's `z` (k01 `f6 t=0 v=z`; iverilog `x`, verilator `0`); (d) a declined callee's body reads are not in `ca_deps`' read set (`levelize/mod.rs`), so its wave can come before the held driver of a net that body reads (r2h2 `f2 t=0 x=1 w1=z`, then `w1=0`; both oracles once, on `0`; PRE ran it on x first); (e) a `$fatal` latched by an assign's call does not stop the rest of that t0 settle (k06; PRE the same); fix for (a): re-run a held uncertified assign only in its own wave when its read set is complete (no declined callee in its calls), per wave otherwise; §4.5.590's round 4 re-ran every one only in its own wave (f07 12 calls, r_chain = PRE) and was reverted: through a declined callee's body read the value stayed stale past t0 (r4a `W2 t=1 w2=0`, r4c, r4d, r4g, g06 `ev u=0 t=1`: events at t ≥ 1 in neither oracle); ### Size cast / signedness

- enum-method-width — an enum method's result (`next()`, `prev()`, `first()`, `last()`, `next(N)`) is 32 bits wide instead of the enum's, so a self-determined use reads it wide: `{2'b11, e.next()}` = `300000002` and `$bits(e.next())` = 32 on a module's own `logic [1:0]` enum variable (fp.enum_width_mod_ctl; iverilog and verilator `e` / `2`), `{~e.next()}` = `fffffffd` (verilator `1`), the same in a class method (fp.enum_width_samemod `cn=02 bn=32`, verilator `0e` / `2`); `num()` is `int` and right; comparisons, `case` and a wider assignment are right by value; the desugar emits untyped integer literals and a ternary chain (`enums.rs` `enum_method_expr`); a `$unit` class's enum property replayed into a module (row 67a's cross-container lane, `extends` and the with-block) keeps it refused until this closes (`class_scope.rs` pins, matrix row67a/enumw: on the `$unit` lane 17 of 42 cells wrong, the in-module and module-variable lanes the same shapes); near §0 10ⓐ (move the enum-method desugar into elaborate); no T-stage row; 2 oracles; OPEN
- Width probe — runs diagnostics and serializes the dead node (E3009 ×2); WALL
- Node width — `ir_bits_of(plain)` is the node's, not the operand's (`2'((s8>>u3)*s16)`); WALL
- Unknown width — `ir_bits_of` → `None` (hier read, string): `2'(u1.mem[0] % 4)` `xx`; WALL
- `typedef enum bit [7:0]` stores 4-state (reads x, keeps `X`; the parser records `enum bit [N]` as `Logic`, `hdl-parser/src/typedefs.rs:351`, and a function returning one gets `ret_two_state` false, `functask.rs:201`: d01rt `repeat (fd(a2))` over a never-assigned `enum bit [3:0]` local `n=2`, d05rt over such a return `n=0`, iverilog and verilator `n=1`, `n=2`; a continuous or procedural write keeps x too, ENG-1c r2 lenses: S12 `e_t y; assign #(1,4) y = e_t'(a);` with `a` 01 → xx → 01 vita `W t=11 y=x \| W t=24 y=1`, iverilog = verilator `W t=14 y=0 \| W t=21 y=1`; E01 undelayed vita `y=xx`, iverilog = verilator `00`; E02 procedural, the same; q4g, and q4g4 a packed struct with an `enum bit` member; q4g5 `y = e_t'(4'bx01z)` vita `y=x01z`, iverilog = verilator `0010`); prerequisite of §3 ⑤ⓕ's enum base and of §2 🆕 AE's `repeat` cut; 2 oracles; OPEN (§5.2 row 8)
- A function returning a base-less enum reads its never-assigned return variable as x at run time (d07rt `repeat (fr(a2))`, `if (fr == 4'd0) fr = B;`: `n=0`, iverilog and verilator `n=2`; the constant fold e07 `n=2` is right; d11, the same return beside a never-assigned `logic [3:0]` local, folds `n=2` and runs `n=0`); the parser records the return 2-state (`functask.rs:201` `ret_two_state`) and a base-less enum local is right (d09rt `n=1`); the run-time site is not found; prerequisite of §2 🆕 AE's `repeat` cut; iverilog + hand-IEEE (§6.19: the base is `int`); OPEN (§5.2 row 8)
- 4-state narrowing drops x (`2'(a+1)` over `8'bxxxx_0011`); iverilog; OPEN
- Call-leaf sign — `64'(f(1) - 40)` zero-extends; give `expr_self_signed`'s `_ => false` the return type; 2 oracles; OPEN

### Constant domain (i64)

- A class field default folds at its own width and keeps x in a 2-state field (`fold_init`, `classes.rs`); 2 oracles; OPEN
- class-scope forward use — a class body is a scope (§4.5.610), but a name binds where it is parsed: a method reading a property declared after it in the class body reads the enclosing scope's variable of that name (c.prop_after_method: vita `ga=0d`, verilator `ga=16`), and a derived class declared before its base without `typedef class` (IEEE-illegal, accepted) gets no base record to replay (rv.ci1: vita `ga=0d`, verilator `ga=16`); the module's own `s.a` is right in both (`a=5`); pinned as recorded known-wrong, not asserted (`class_scope.rs` `class_scope_forward_use_keeps_the_module_variable`); verilator; OPEN
- inline-constraint-caller-scope — a name in `h.randomize() with { … }` that `h`'s class record does not bind is read where the call stands, not in the class (IEEE 1800-2017 §18.7): a class `bit [7:0] P` beside a module `localparam logic [1:0][3:0] P` gives `x == P[1]` the module's flat bit (q3.rw_md_param `x=00`; `k.P[1]` = 1), a class `int W` beside a module `localparam int W = 4` gives `x == W` the module's 4 (q3.rw_const; `k.W` = 9); no tool oracle here (iverilog has no inline `with`, verilator no constraint solver on this host); hand-IEEE; OPEN
- A packed multi-dimensional class property reads an element select as the flat bit: `p[1]` on a class `logic [1:0][3:0] p = 8'hA5` is `0` in a method (n.md_prop_ctl; verilator `a`; the module-scope twin is right); and a packed array of struct property's member select in a method is `xX` (q5.cls_parr_own `s[1].a`; verilator `16`); PRE = POST (§4.5.610 review); verilator; OPEN
- A class extending a class declared in another module is accepted (q2.two_modules: `ga=0d a=5`; verilator `Class for 'extends' not found`); accept-invalid; verilator; OPEN

### Scoping / imports / block-locals

- struct-binding-by-name — since §4.5.614 (§5.2 row 67b) a variable keeps the declaration it bound where it was declared (`decl_key.rs`), but WHICH declaration a type NAME denotes there is still read from name-keyed maps that a re-declaration of another kind does not clear (an outer or imported packed struct, per-instance struct or record stays under the name, `var_struct_ref` takes them in the member reads' order — packed struct, then packable record, then per-instance struct — so a record or per-instance binding binds an outer packed entry of the name; row 67b's lens cells t4, t8), so a variable of the INNER type is laid out by the outer one at exit 0: a vector typedef over a unit or imported struct (a1u, a3u `r=06`, iverilog and sv2v → iverilog `r=12`; k01_u `f=06`, iverilog `f=12`; x.cat.c571alias `w=42`, all three `w=12`), a struct laid out per instance (a2u `r=001`, both `r=101`; a31u `o=001f`, verilator and iverilog `o=01ff`; f4.blk.P / fn / gif / task `w=21`, both `w=42`); a generate block's per-instance `st_t` under a module packed `st_t`, t8.sym_vs_packed `r=0d`, iverilog, sv2v → iverilog and verilator `16`), a type parameter or a `[1:0][3:0]` vector (b13u, a11f `v=42`, verilator and sv2v → iverilog `v=12`), an unpacked record (k06_u, u01_u `a=09 b=3`, sv2v → iverilog `a=9 b=7`; f4.blk.R `w=21`, verilator `w=42`; a block-local record `st_t` under a module packed `st_t`, t4.record_shadows_packed `r=0a`, verilator `2a`), a module vector typedef under a unit record (x.cat.c573 `b=10 v=3ff`, sv2v → iverilog and verilator `b=8 v=ff`), an alias of a name an unpacked record shadows, which copies the shadowed struct (h01e `bits=8`, verilator `16`; o46_u, o46f_u, o46g_u `a=09 b=3`, sv2v → iverilog `a=9 b=7`, `a=9 b=27`), a `$bits` / cast of a vector or enum re-declared over a struct (f5.fn.V6.bits `q=8`, iverilog and verilator `q=6`; f5.gif.V6.cast, E6 alike; f5.gif.tparam), a nested member typed by a name a vector re-declares (rv.n1 `q=5`, an invalid `o.m.a` accepted; verilator refuses), and a plain formal or port named like a module struct or record variable, which does not unbind it (x.cat.c571formal `g=42`, all three `g=12`; rv.fr2 `f=05`, verilator `f=16`; x.formal_plain_gen accepted, both refuse); PRE = POST for every cell here (§4.5.614 measured); dropping the outer layout at the re-declaration breaks the OUTER variables instead (§5.2 row 36 review round 2: g05b `f=12` where PRE and verilator print `02`), so the fix decides the kind and the declaration a name denotes where it stands from one newest-registration funnel (row 66's type record, design v2 P3: the hide rule and the formal-only unbind); 3 oracles; OPEN
- declaration-order-accept — a type name used before the same scope declares or imports another type of that name binds the earlier declaration (IEEE 1800-2017 §26.3; §4.5.592 takes Icarus Verilog as the oracle on this axis), which IEEE 1800-2023 §6.18 makes illegal: verilator 5.052 refuses each (`Reference to 'st' before declaration (IEEE 1800-2023 6.18)`) and vita accepts it at exit 0 — u.mod_redecl_after `a=5` (iverilog `a=5`, sv2v → iverilog `a=16`), u.mod_redecl_after_pat `s=34` (iverilog and sv2v → iverilog `34`), u.mod_redecl_after_kpat `s=34` (hand-IEEE; sv2v → iverilog `03`, iverilog refuses the keyed pattern), rv.q3b `w=05`, rv.q3c `w=05` (iverilog `05`, sv2v → iverilog `16`), rv.q3d `q=05` (iverilog), g.cls_then_mod_redecl `ga=16 ta=d` (PRE the same) and g.cls_then_mod_redecl_derived `ga=16` (E3010 before §4.5.614; iverilog and sv2v refuse the classes); accept-invalid under 2023 only; refusing it would descend under 1800-2017; verilator; OPEN
- union-member-pattern — an untyped nested `'{…}` for a packed-union member concatenates every member of the overlay and is right only where the target truncates the surplus away: b11gu `'{c: 4'h7, u: '{4'h5, 4'h6}}` `r=56`, verilator and sv2v → iverilog `r=76`; n01_u `r=57` (sv2v → iverilog `57`, iverilog `67`); b11u `r=67`, b11f `r=57` and r8a's array initializer are right by truncation (= verilator and sv2v → iverilog); PRE = POST; the typed pattern refuses it (§4.5.609); fix: lower the nested value against the member's own width; OPEN
- typed-pattern-class-name — a module-local `class s_t` shadowing a unit packed struct `s_t` leaves the struct's typedef entry under the name (a class name is registered by a prescan that runs before every typedef and never overrides one), so `s_t'{4'h3, 4'h1}` is accepted (k06 `w=31`) where verilator rejects the source (`Assignment pattern applies against non-struct/union data type: 'class{}s_t'`); accept-invalid; fix: give a class declaration a registration where it is declared (`TypeInfo::bind`, §4.5.609) so the newest-registration rule refuses it; OPEN
- bits-record-shadow — `$bits(T)` of an unpacked record that shadows an outer packed struct `T` (a generate block or a function declaring `typedef struct { logic [7:0] a; logic [7:0] b; } s_t;` under a module's 8-bit `struct packed` `s_t`) returns the outer struct's width (k13 `b1=8 b2=8`, verilator `b1=16 b2=16`; k05, k05b, k09, k12 alike); PRE = POST; the record writes no typedef entry, so the name's stale one answers (struct-binding-by-name's root); OPEN
- A generate block's `event N` shadowing an outer `localparam int N = 0` read as a constant width runs silently (t.event_blk: `case (3) P[0 +: N]:` takes `default`, `o=22`) where all three oracles refuse; the constant fold reads the outer parameter (`lookup_scoped`); pre-existing (PRE = row 65); 3 oracles; OPEN
- `$bits` reads the flat decl-order prescan (`bits_prescan`, `bits_of_view`) before the scope-aware net table in every nested scope, so a generate block's, a routine's, a begin-block's or an interface's own declaration answers with a same-named outer one (an interface body reads its instantiating module's): z01 gen whole `A=32`, z02 function-local, z03 block-local (all three 16), z04 gen unpacked view 32 (8), z05 gen replication `ffffffff` (`ff`), z06 gen 1-D vs module unpacked 8 (1), f13 and d32 block-local unpacked vs module localparam 1 (8), d11 gen shadow 8 (4), i04 interface `B=32` (16), a07 fork-local `01` (Verilator `ff`), a19, a27, a29, a35, a44 typedef-at-use over a nested constant 1-2 (8-16), a22 concurrent-assertion action `01`; PRE=POST (§4.5.605's select arms decline in a nested scope and do nothing in an interface body); the B-stage fix applies the scope gate to every prescan read; OPEN
- `pk.hf(x)` on an instance named like a package resolves to the package (`inline_fn.rs`, `expr_size_ctx.rs::pkg_call_head`); verilator; OPEN
- A routine imported into another package binds in the importer or the caller, never in its own package: `elaborate_package`'s import arm files `r::g` in `pkg_funcs[p]`, which `pkg_owns` does not own, so §4.5.589's window stays closed: a call to it from `p`'s routine text keeps the caller's binding (n_pkgimp `P=232 v=232`, pi_rfn_hdr `232`; both oracles `8`), its body run from `p`'s body reads `p`'s constants (c12 `L=9 v=9`; iverilog, verilator, sv2v `L=3 v=3`), and its default is evaluated with `p`'s (ifn_dflt `v=3`, kx_tdflt_imp `u=3`; iverilog `8`); record the origin at the import arm (`imp.pkg`) and key the window, the interpreter's package tag and the default lane on it; 2 oracles; OPEN (§5.2 row 2, shipping with row 45)
- A package's own function reading a constant its package imported binds the name in the caller (row 39's review, p07c: PRE `f=2`, iverilog and sv2v → iverilog `f=7`; an exported constant reaches it the same way, p07); 2 oracles; OPEN
- A package body's read after a shadowing block falls to the caller; BLOCKED (binding-resolved scope)
- A `fork`'s own declarations (`fork logic [7:0] x; … join`) take the bare-name flatten with W3056 (`stmt_main.rs` warns them ignored, `gather_auto_block_locals` admits no fork span), so beside a module `x` the fork writes the module's (fl_ctl `inner=000000aa outer=000000aa`, fl_bits run-time `$bits` `r=01`; iverilog, sv2v → iverilog and verilator `inner=aa outer=11111111`, `ff`); under a timing control the same since §4.5.611 (fl_d); 3 oracles; OPEN
- A declaring block in a procedural deferred assertion's action (`assert #0 (c) else begin logic [7:0] x; … end`) is accepted and not hoisted (`block_local::for_each_nested_stmt` does not enter assertion actions): its writes land on the module's `x` (x_dassert_ctl `inner=000000aa outer=000000aa`; verilator `inner=aa outer=11111111`; IEEE 1800-2017 §16.4 allows each action of a deferred assertion one subroutine call; iverilog rejects `assert #0`, sv2v drops it); verilator + hand-IEEE; OPEN
- A package task's call of a sibling package TASK is not injected with it: `import p::a;` where `a` calls `b` is E3010 `call to undeclared task b` (cc8), and beside a module task `b` it runs the module's (cc1, cc9 `b=mod`; iverilog, sv2v → iverilog and verilator `b=pkg`); a sibling function is injected, also from a timing control's body since §4.5.613; 3 oracles; OPEN
- A block-local declared under a timing control in a task or function body is E3009 since §4.5.611 (row 72's re-plan, option (b)). Before, the declaration was dropped and its name bound the task's or the caller's same-named variable (T27: a module `integer i` loop driving a task whose `@(posedge clk) begin integer i; …` loops: `n=4 i=5`, iverilog, sv2v → iverilog and verilator `n=12 i=3`; T04 `x=3` for `x=7`, T07 `outer=000000aa`, T19 `mc=9` for `3`, T21 `a=0b` for `ab`, k10 `cnt=1`, k20 `t=23` for `t=6`) or was E3010. Refused also where that binding happened to give the right value — ruled (re-plan S0) as right only by accident, each one edit from a wrong value on PRE: T03, T05, T09, T11, T14, T15, T24, T30, k01, k02, k03, t_later_m, v6 (twins T04, T06, T09b, T12, T14b, T15b, T24b, t_later_mc, s6); ruled after round 5's differential review: t01, t02, o16, X8_formal_auto_emu, X8_sib_auto_emu, X8_sib_static_emu (twins t01w `outer=aa`, t02w `b=4`, o16w and o16v `i=2`, X8_formal_static_emu `s=1 proc i=3`, X8_sib_auto_w and X8_sib_static_w `s=5`; all three oracles `outer=11`, `b=0`, `i=7`, `s=3 proc i=50`, `s=1`); and from earlier rounds pk9, pk13, pk28, au_in, fs1. Prerequisite: §5.2 row 72b; four review rounds on the name axis (r1 F5, r2 G1, r3 K1/K2, r4 M1/M3/N1; W/row72/review); 3 oracles; OPEN (§5.2 row 72b)
- A static task's (or `function void`'s), a package routine's (static or automatic, task or function) and a `let`'s body is lowered inside the calling block, so a free name it shares with a block-local of that block binds the local: b7_plain `a=9`, b7d_plain (`#(z)`) `a=2`, b7w_plain (the task writes `z`) `b=7 mz=5`, b7n_plain, b7g (`always @(posedge clk) begin int z;`), b6_plain (an imported package variable `pv=5 a=9`), r8_plain / r8a_plain / r8f_plain (a package task, automatic task or function reading a name its package imports, `a=9`), l2_plain / l1b_plain (a `let`, `a=9`); iverilog, sv2v → iverilog and verilator `a=5`, `a=5`, `b=9 mz=7`, `a=5`, verilator alone `a=5` for `let`. Allow-listed as non-capturing for their own body (rounds 5–6, every free-name kind): a module `automatic` task, a module non-void function, a module static task reached only through an `automatic` task, a hierarchical call into a child or an interface. Under a timing control the local is E3009 since §4.5.611 wherever its block reaches — by name mention, closed through every routine including the allow-listed ones — a callable that is not allow-listed (module static task, package routine, `let`, class, sequence, property, covergroup) whose definition names it; the message gives the call path (P1 of round 5's review: b7, b7d, b7w, b7f, b17, b19, b22; F1 / F2 of round 6's: r8, r8a, r8f, r8c, r8x, r8d, r8w, l1, l1b, l2, l3). Fail-closed, so names that happened to read right are refused too: K_enum, K_genvar, b8, b8e, b9, K_range_only, K_hier_first_seg; a package routine's own package variable (L9, A12, b16); a `let` reached through an allow-listed lane (A6, A7, A8, l4); the round-7 differential review's package and `let` idioms (NOTE 2): a package routine's formal or own local named like the local (p01, p02, p02c, p03), the package's own variable (p01c), a `let` formal (p04) — PRE loud on each except p01c, p01u and p07b (PRE wrong); ruled (main): p07a, right before the row only by accident (twin p07b `modi=4`, oracles `modi=2`). The path the message prints is a chain of names, each mentioned in the text before it, not a proven call (p05, p06c, p08e). Over-refused as well, and refused on PRE too: a local named like a name the static task declares itself (diff lens round 6 NOTE 1: i02, i02a, i02n, i02t, i02u, i02v, i02y, i11, i11t, c6, c6t, c6u, c6v, c6w, c6x) — §5.2 row 72b. B stage (§5.2 row 72b); 3 oracles (verilator for `let`); OPEN
- A static task binds an input argument by substitution over its whole body, so a block-local of the argument's name reads and writes the argument (pk10_ctl `a=7`, pk11_ctl `g=257`, pk14_ctl package task, pk17_ctl generate-block task; iverilog, sv2v → iverilog and verilator `a=3`, `g=16`; `inline_task.rs` `subst`); an `automatic` task's block-local named like an input argument shares its slot (pk12_ctl `g=16`, oracles `g=0`; pk8_ctl `in=7`, oracles `0`); 3 oracles; OPEN
- A static task's `automatic` block-local is one copy for every call: its initializer runs once, at the first call (`inline_task_locals.rs` `first_call`; no per-entry lane for subroutine bodies): lt17_ctl `c=6 7 8`, lt2b_ctl `y=10 10 10`, lt13_ctl package task, lt15_ctl generate-block task (sv2v → iverilog and verilator `6 6 6`, `10 20 30`; iverilog refuses the lifetime), and a read before its write sees the previous call's value (lt11_ctl `c=0 5`, verilator `0 0`); the module lane refuses the same shapes (E3009); 2 oracles; OPEN
- A static task's static block-local initialised from a run-time value (an argument, a module variable) runs the initializer at the first call, not once before time 0 (lt10_ctl `y=10 11 12`, iverilog `0 1 2`; si_mod_ctl `y=50 51`, verilator `10 11`, iverilog `0 1`); the frame lane's twin runs it at each call (rec_si_ctl `y=20 10 0`, iverilog `0 1 2`; the HELD line `frame_static_init_t0_safe` below); 2 oracles; OPEN

- An `automatic` task's block-local named like an INPUT argument shares the argument's slot, and a delay, event or `iff` expression outside the block that reads the argument sees the block's write (the no-timing twin k20c of k20 `repeat (2) #(k) begin int k; k = 20; … end`, whose PRE value is `t=23` where iverilog, sv2v → iverilog and verilator print `t=6`; k10 `iff k > 0` `cnt=1`, verilator `cnt=2`; the timing-body forms are E3009 since §4.5.611): `check_block_local_scope_leaks` walks statements, not timing-control expressions — extend it there (§5.2 row 72b, c4); 3 oracles; OPEN

### Delays / events

- ① An `automatic` loop-body local read by a `fork … join_none` arm's delay (`for (…) begin automatic int id = i; fork begin #(id+1) $display(id); end join_none end`, a common testbench idiom) gives every arm the last value (w09 `id=2 t=3` three times; verilator `id=0 t=1 | id=1 t=2 | id=2 t=3`; iverilog refuses the lifetime): one flattened net per `automatic`; the fork-local form (w08) is E3009; verilator; OPEN
- ① `$assertoff`, `$asserton` and `$assertkill` reach only the concurrent checkers' reports: a simple immediate, deferred or final assertion's pass and fail actions and its default `$error` keep running while assertions are off (row 19 A14 t2h procedural twins: `Fe t=5`, `Ff t=5`, the default `Assertion failed`; Verilator prints none), its condition is still evaluated (E25 `chk v=-1 t=5`, E29 an `$error` inside the condition's function; Verilator `end`), a system-read action (`$sscanf`, `$value$plusargs`, `$random(seed)`) still writes its destination (G1), and a work-library unit compiled on its own is never gated (R2-4, Q2_worklib `F t=5`); §4.5.606 refuses only a module-item deferred assertion beside a control call; fix in §5.2 row 73; OPEN
- A concurrent assertion's own pass or fail action (`assert property (@(posedge clk) a) else $display(…)`) keeps running while assertions are off; only the synthesized default `$error` is gated (`sva_check.rs`, `in_assert_synth`): `Fc t=25`, `Fc t=35` between `$assertoff` at 22 and `$asserton` at 42, Verilator none (IEEE 1800-2017 §20.11); §5.2 row 73's gate; OPEN
- An in-body `@(*)` over a dyn handle stays stale (compares the handle word); iverilog; OPEN
- A `wait` over a heap container's element or method never wakes when the container's elements are written — queues, dynamic arrays and associative arrays alike: w9_queue_size `wait (q.size() == 2)` after two `push_back`, w9_dyn_elem `wait (d[1] == 5)` after `d[1] = 5`, w9_assoc_elem_typed `wait (mem[16] == 8'h5a)` on `[bit [31:0]]`, r4_wait_val_ew / r4_wait_num_ew / r4_wait_exists_ew over `bit [7:0] mem [int]` (`mem[16]`, `mem.num() == 2`, `mem.exists(16)`) each print only `end`, no diagnostic, on PRE 77231d42 (verilator `woke t=5` for each, `woke t=5 n=2` / `woke t=5 v=5a` for the `$readmemh` twins r4_wait_num / r4_wait_exists, `t=5 n=2 5a` for sens_wake): the engine re-checks a `WaitCause::Expr` waiter after a net change (`sched/propagate.rs`), and an element write to heap storage is not one; `always_comb`, `always @*`, `assign` and `$monitor` over the same reads do wake. Since §4.5.617 a `$readmem*` into an associative array that any `wait` condition reads (directly, through a method or an element, hierarchically, or inside a called function) is refused by name (E3009) rather than opened onto this; prerequisite candidate for that refusal and for every heap-container `wait`: a heap write wakes the expression waiters that read its handle; verilator; OPEN
- An associative array declared in a static task's body is one word: `task t; bit [7:0] mem [int]; mem[16] = 8'h5a; mem[17] = 8'ha5; mem[3] = 8'h33;` reads `v=xx xx xx` with an E4002 warning per access (`array word index of top.$itask$t$L.mem … out of range`), and `$readmemh("p.hex", mem)` there warns W4023 `address 16 outside the load range` and reads `v=xx xx` (n2_task_local_ew, n2_task_local = lens e8_static_task_local2; verilator `v=5a a5 33`, `v=5a a5`; the same on PRE 77231d42); the static task's body-local takes the inline-task lane (`inline_task_locals.rs` `hoist_one_inline_local`), which has no dynamic-storage handle arm and sizes the `[int]` dimension through `array_dim_extents` as one word (`array_geom.rs`: `Dim::Dyn | Dim::Queue(_) | Dim::Assoc(_) => (0, 1)`, commented "dyn dims never reach the static-extent path"); verilator; OPEN

### Diagnostics / artifacts

- ① `$strobe` / `$monitor` of an `automatic` task's variable panics (`frame read: no active call window`): k14b, k14c, k14d with no timing control; k14e and k24, under one, are E3009 since §4.5.611 (they were E3010). A static task's inline lane and a re-spawned arm print the shared storage instead (k12 `x=11 x=11`, w04 `x=1 x=1`). IEEE 1800 §13.3.2 bars tracing an automatic with `$monitor` (text unverified); verilator internal errors, iverilog refuses the lifetime; no oracle; OPEN
- A zero-width refusal names the width "zero" where a generate block's typedef, class, interface or module instance, or function shadows the width's name and the fold reads the outer `localparam int N = 0` (t.typedef_label_blk, t.class_mod_unit, t.iface_inst_blk, t.mod_inst_blk, t.fn_bare_label_blk, t.fn_bare_decl_blk; row 65 review round 3): all three oracles refuse with a type or kind error (iverilog "Type names are not valid expressions here", "Unable to bind parameter"; verilator "Data type used where a non-data type is expected", "FUNC but expected a variable", "CELL but expected a variable"); the claim's any-kind check (`edge_gate.rs` `binding_encloses`) reads the constant tables and `symbols` only; loud either way, the reason is wrong; 3 oracles; OPEN
- A part-select of a hierarchical net declared ascending whose bounds fold only after elaboration is E3009 "bounds out of order" although they are in order (rb03 `u.g2[gb.A:gb.B]`, rb16; verilator and sv2v `3e000000`): the deferred lane builds its width before the net's direction is known (`width_from_msb_lsb_checked`), and the literal twin's refusal says the net is descending (rb03b); fix: name the ascending hierarchical net in both refusals, and build the width in its direction when the lane resolves it; OPEN
- A replication count above 2^24 repeats 2^24 times (`sim_ir::selfwidth::WIDTH_MAX`; `{(32'd0-32'd1){1'b1}}`): an assignment's low bits are right (pin `an_unsigned_wrap_count_still_replicates`), the replication's own width is not; RECORD
- A Mul chain base re-lowered n times emits n E4002 copies; OPEN
- `coverpoint_domain`'s Pow arm uses `max(lw,rw)`; OPEN
- `run.json` omits `-G` (§6 OBS-1); OPEN
- A CA's user function runs more than once per event; BLOCKED (purity-certification adjudication)
- An operator over a call names it twice (`p8() >>> 1`); BLOCKED (purity-certification adjudication)
- `wprog` decline reasons relabelled by §4.5.530; RECORD
- `%h` of a 1-bit unknown expression is `x` (iverilog `X`, inconsistent); RECORD
- A string literal's NUL byte is kept raw (oracles a space); 2 oracles; OPEN
- `$dumpfile(DF)` names the file after the parameter path; iverilog; OPEN
- `V.x` under a shadowing constant gives the generic E3010 (`bare_const_shadows_net`); OPEN

### Oracle splits (recorded, not chased)

- an explicit import after a wildcard import of the same type name (`import p::*; import q::s_t;`, n02, §4.5.609 review) — verilator binds `s_t` to p's type (`A o=09`), sv2v → iverilog to q's (`A o=95`, as Icarus Verilog does on the untyped twin); vita = sv2v and iverilog (PRE untyped and POST typed `A o=95`); split
- a `let` declaration shadowing an outer parameter read as a constant width (t.let_label_blk, t.let_decl_mod_unit; row 65 review round 3) — no oracle (iverilog sorry, sv2v a parse error, verilator an internal error or "VAR but expected a task/function"): PRE reads the outer parameter, also at run time (`n=0`), and row 65's zero-width claim refuses it as zero
- a negative SIGNED width operand (`f[0 +: -4'sd2]`) — iverilog, sv2v and verilator read its 4-bit pattern as 14 (`0def`); vita refuses it as negative (E3009, §4.5.601 RC1), as it refuses `f[0 +: -1]` (iverilog and sv2v read one bit, verilator refuses); loud, recorded
- a static function's local read before it is written in a loop body, across calls at time 0 (row-25 lens r2 d04 `g[2*i +: 2] = t; t = a[2*i +: 2];`, frame lane) — vita (PRE, §4.5.615) and verilator `z=01110010` then `z=10001101`, iverilog and sv2v → iverilog `z=011100xx` then `z=10001110` (the call count of a static function at time 0); split
- select vs whole-net level waiter order — split (vita = verilator)
- deferred-assertion report maturity — no oracle (vita hand-IEEE §16.4)
- a real stored into a string — no oracle (vita the element rule)
- t0 wake / edge of a no-definite-bit driver — split (vita by value, §9.4.2)
- zero-delay CA update visibility — split (vita: first `#0` promotion)
- t0 Active order around an all-constant `always @(K)` — split
- what runs after `$finish` in its step — split
- packed dims before an unpacked typedef name — split (vita = verilator)
- init width of untyped `4'd15 + 4'd1` — split (vita = iverilog 16)
- 64-bit unsigned `%` — iverilog self-contradicts
- cross-scope t0 decl-init race; iverilog expression-force — do not chase (runtime `-0.0` events: §4.5.618, its rendering: §4.5.619; the rest is ENG-1b, ENG-1c)
- a delayed continuous assignment re-evaluated with identical NaN bits after another operand moved (Q-NANDCA, ENG-1 r2 D13) — vita lands at the old time (`t=7`), iverilog and sv2v reschedule (`t=8`), verilator does not converge: vita re-decides a delayed right-hand side on settle passes without an operand change, so identical NaN bits are one value (`value::real_values_same`, ENG-1c) where IEEE 754 says `NaN != NaN`; the same at step (c) (ENG-1c mutant N5, pin `d13c`: the right-hand side returns to the landed NaN bits while `1.0` is pending — vita drops the pending value, `W t=3 | F t=12`; iverilog = sv2v `W t=3 | W t=13 | F t=13`; verilator does not converge; the non-NaN control k1: vita = iverilog = sv2v t=12); following iverilog needs per-assign operand-change tracking; recorded split
- an integral delayed continuous assignment's rise / fall — vita and iverilog take the largest delay among the changing bits (`sched/wait_fork.rs` `transition_delay`), verilator the whole-vector rule of IEEE 1800 §10.3.3 (ieee1800-2017.txt:10342-10345): T07 first drive vita = iverilog `W t=13`, verilator `t=2`; Q1p `-1 -> 1` sign-extended into 8 bits vita = iverilog `t=21`, verilator `t=24`; F2c, F2e3; split (vita = iverilog)
- z into a `real` destination under `#(rise, fall, turnoff)` (`assign #(1,4,2) b = i;`, `i` 5 -> `'z`, ENG-1c O1) — no oracle (iverilog rises on every real transition, `t=11`; verilator never lands the z): vita stores 0.0 and takes the fall delay (`t=14`), the value rule every real destination follows; PRE took the turn-off delay (`t=12`); hand-IEEE
- `$stime` sign under a cast — split (vita unsigned, 1364 §17.7.2)
- cross-package const-function recursion — vita = hand-IEEE
- `#(.S("str"))` prints one spurious W3056 (value right)
- untyped `K - 20` over `bit [3:0]` — split (vita = verilator 32 bits)
- real beside a bit-vector inline region — split
- import precedence — verilator binds the first import of a name, explicit or wildcard (D21 `import pb::P; import pa::*;` the explicit `P`; D22 and X1 the wildcard's; Iab_sel, D40 the first of two wildcards); iverilog, sv2v and §26.3 let an explicit import win wherever it is written and refuse two wildcards: verilator is no oracle there
- forward references in a generate scope — not a split (§4.5.592): IEEE 1800-2017 §26.3 binds a reference other than a call only to a declaration before it, else in the next outer scope, else it is illegal (its Example 1: "line 2 initializes p::x. Line 4 initializes top.b.x."), and iverilog follows it (``Unable to bind parameter `K' in `top.gb'`` … `Check for declaration after use.`); sv2v and verilator bind scope-wide and contradict the example (Z0a, Example 1 without its illegal line: iverilog `px=1 bx=2`, sv2v `px=z bx=2`, verilator `px=0 bx=2`; parameter twin Z0b: iverilog `l2 X=1`, both others `l2 X=2`): they are disqualified on this axis, so a forward-reference cell is decided by iverilog and §26.3, never by their majority
- a static inline task's body-local enum label beside a same-named module >64-bit, string or real constant (Ef*_tk2) — split (iverilog the module constant, verilator the label `r=1`, sv2v cannot parse); vita takes the label beside a narrow constant and the module constant beside the others (`r=0`, `16706`, `3`)
- use before declaration in an interface — loud kept (= iverilog)
- `#(.P(pk::PA + 0))` over `[0:35]` — split (`pkg_const_layout_override.rs`)
- `%s` of an unstored `string'(e)` — split (vita = verilator)
- string-cast spellings with ≤1 oracle — stay loud
- `always @*` at t0 (no pass until an input changes, = iverilog and §9.2.2.2; verilator runs it) and reading only a constant — split
- unlabelled `begin` in a generate region — split (vita = iverilog)
- unlabelled generate net beside a same-named function — split (vita = iverilog)
- repeated non-ANSI header port — split
- non-finite / out-of-range real into an integral — split
- override equal to the default: verilator is no oracle there
- `u.w[u.P*2-1:0]` — split (iverilog rejects)
- `$bits(u.r)` of a hierarchical real — split (vita = verilator 64)
- constant term beside a live one, suspending body — split (vita = verilator)
- constant term beside an edge term — split (vita = iverilog)
- in-body wait on a constant — split (vita = verilator)
- named event beside a constant — split (vita = verilator)
- constant equal to a variable's default as an event term — split
- t0 NBA / `#0` write of a live term — split (vita = iverilog)
- same-step glitch under an in-body wait — split (vita = verilator)
- net-only header level list at t0 — split
- same-time resume order (wake group, fork arms, t0 hierarchy, `wire #0`) — split
- `$strobe` before the first monitored change — split (vita = verilator)
- `-G` decimal beyond 32-bit signed — split
- sign keyword with no range beside a real — split
- `$realtobits` of an integral — split (`integral_to_f64`)
- package-body enum label named like a constant — split
- negative signed part-select bound — split; vita mixes both readings
- `always_comb` / `always_latch` t0 passes of several blocks — split (vita forward source order, one per batch with a settle after each; iverilog reverse source order; verilator dependency order): from a quiet source a forward chain reports at t0 in iverilog only (tg1, tg3, tg4, tg6, tg8, tg10, tg13, tg14), a consumer-first chain in vita only (§2 🆕 AA)
- a constant or declaration-fed `always_comb` read at t0 before its pass (an `initial`'s first slice, a reader another t0 write woke, a t0-posedge flop: q_const_comb_first, s583_v01, s583_v03, x3, r1, r2, f2, f2b) — split (vita = iverilog `xx`; verilator the value; after one `#0` vita reads the value, iverilog `xx`)
- `always_comb` run count at t0 when an input changes at t0 — split (vita = iverilog twice: the woken pass, then the implicit one; verilator once; d1b iverilog once)
- GATED-CLOCK edge dedup at t0 (`reset_edge_seen_marks` now runs at t0 in every comb design): an edge from a settle-woken comb pass is deduped, one from an implicit pass re-fires (g1) — split (vita = iverilog on the woken pass's edge, neither on the implicit pass's)
- an implicit t0 pass's wakes against a promoted `#0` resume (c3, c4: iverilog `s=x g=x`, verilator `s=1 g=1`, vita `s=1 g=x`; fj1, a fork arm's `#0`) — split; vita matches neither (a legal §4.7 order)
- comb t0 pass slots in a port chain (t3g: vita = verilator, iverilog reports at t0), a first-batch-woken checker reading an implicit-only comb (t3k: vita = iverilog's two W4031) and a named event a comb's t0 pass triggers (n1: vita = iverilog `E1 t=0 y=01`, verilator nothing) — split
- a self-timed `always` (no header) written before the `initial` that drives it, at t0 — split (vita = iverilog: source order, a `unique` miss on x reports at t0; verilator starts the `initial` first): s1s_case (iverilog `Time: 0`, `Time: 1`), s3_case (`Time: 0`, `Time: 2`), s1s_selftimed_first, s3_child_selftimed_tbL (the `if` chains, §4.5.585), s583 v04
- a t0 read of a held continuous assign's output, or of an assign below it, before the release (an `initial`'s first slice): vita `z` on a net, `x` on a variable (hand-IEEE §6.8); iverilog `z`; verilator the value from the declared defaults when it runs the assign first (a6, a7 `i0 y=01`) or 2-state `0` when it runs the `initial` first (a2 `i0 y=00`) — split; both tools order a continuous assign and an `initial` both ways in other designs (T2a, a2, T3), and an assign with only constant arguments keeps PRE's place before the processes (a4 = verilator; iverilog after) — `t0_call_cont_assign_hold.rs` race pins

- pkg-task-stmt — same defect as `pkg-scoped-task`; DUP
- blocal-inert-falseloud — an inert block-local is refused (`check_block_local_scope_leaks`); BLOCKED (binding-resolved scope)

- reduction-init — `$sformatf` over `arr.sum()` in a declaration is E3009; OPEN
- string-array — fixed string array init, runtime index, `string q[$]`, 2-D; iverilog; OPEN
- inline-string — a static task's inline string local is E3018; OPEN
- string-misc — `s[i:j]`, `s[i].len()`, string queue / assoc elements, `u.q[0]`; OPEN
- dyn-md-elem-select — a select into a md element of a dyn array is E3009; record the handle's packed extents (`netdecl.rs`), route via `lower_packed_read`; verilator; OPEN
- class-field-select — `c.u8[3:0]` is E3010; route the select's base through the class-field read; verilator; OPEN

**VCD / real conversion**

- vcd — cosmetic VCD encoding differences (widths, t0 dump, var kinds); iverilog; OPEN
- const-x-real — a constant with an x/z bit converted to real is E3009, where the run-time conversion (§4.5.599) and iverilog read each x/z bit as 0: `localparam real P = 4'bx011;` ("value is not a constant: 4'bx011 has no constant-fold arm"), `#(.P(4'bx011))` onto a real parameter ("a real override cannot be folded"), `localparam int W = $rtoi(4'bx011 * 1.0);`, `localparam real P = 4'bx011; if (P > 2.0)`; iverilog, verilator and sv2v → iverilog `P=3.000000`, `bits=4`, `gen=hit`; `const_real.rs` `const_selfdet_real` and the override's unknown-bit filter (`params.rs`) decline; 3 oracles; OPEN

### 3.c Intentionally loud (not gaps)

- `$value$plusargs` in an arbitrary expression — no statement form to keep single evaluation
- `%p` of `int a[0:0]` — the IR carries no array-ness
- a direction mismatch on an unpacked array port (`[0:3]` against `[3:0]`) — §7.6 pairs by position
- a fixed-array fill whose coverage cannot be proved (computed index, conditional write, incomplete set) — a rule
- calling the same dyn-formal function twice, or recursively, in one frame-body expression — one marker slot
- shadowing by a block that encloses a block and redeclares the same name, when NO module-scope net carries that name — two initialisations on one net
- a `$readmem*` child-versus-parent `initial` race — split
- `$readmemh` into a `wire` array — split
- a header default that names a constant from a body import — split
- two iverilog 13.0 defects (vita is IEEE-correct) — `break` after a loop local; `continue` in a `case`
- `%l` — documented choice (`%u` / `%z` → §5.2 P-PCTU)

## 4. SVA / verification honest-loud residues

- `##0` fused with `##[m:$]`; BLOCKED (§16.9.2.1 discontinuity)
- N2c full sequence local variables; BLOCKED (nested-attempt data model)
- later-antecedent read and outer `|=>` skew; BLOCKED (2-cycle / cross-clock census)
- SVA-QUAD collapse default flip (`VITA_SVA_COLLAPSE`); BLOCKED (full-VCD golden audit)
- N4 clocking skew value (`output #0`); BLOCKED (hand-IEEE §14.11 / §14.16)
- class casts `Derived'(base)`, `(B'(d)).foo()`; BLOCKED (a `$cast` type guard)

## 5. Performance / hardening

Below the correctness ladder; a row resumes only when its re-entry condition fires. `§5.1-<x>` records: `git show acabe991:docs/history/ROADMAP_ARCHIVE_PHASE_A-D.md`.

### 5.a Standing verdicts

| id | verdict | reason | re-entry condition |
|---|---|---|---|
| codegen (cranelift), including the `§5.1-be` machine-code experiment | rejected | the call boundary (~38% of a run) exceeds the 8.9–11.3% ceiling; most `wprog` programs are one op | inline leaf loads and 2-state arithmetic without writing the semantics twice |
| D2-b two-state storage | rejected | a step down the accuracy ladder | a way with no correctness trade |
| cycle-based mode | rejected | event-driven already skips ~90% of combinational work | real demand with ≥1 evaluation per block per cycle |
| levelize (rank-ordered Active drain) | discarded | 1.00×; the dirty settle closed the root | none |
| process fusion (the E axis) | not adopted | a chain reader sees the propagated value (silent-wrong) | none |
| net-count reduction (flatten) | on hold | erases what `--probe`, VCD, `%m` and hierarchical names target | an owner ruling |
| S4 schedule elision · S5 NBA specialisation | stopped | below the 1.3× stop threshold | none |
| the `wprog` reject family inside settle | not started | ~2–2.5% of serv; needs `Tern` as a jump | prize exceeds the machinery |
| `drain_range_diags` early-out | rejected | zero gain | none |

### 5.b Open performance and hardening residues

- 4b-r — native does not pool scratch (`fire_waiters`, `settle_cont_assigns` `vals`); OPEN
- 5c — a frame body never enters `wprog` (`Vec<Value>` window; `arena.frame` declines); a flat word window; BLOCKED (arena)
- ARR-LHS — md element LHS ~10× cliff (= §2 row 19); BLOCKED (its own census first)
- INLINE-FOLD — the inline fold is exponential (DAG walked as a tree); per-activation memoisation; OPEN
- MEM-GUARD — no process memory guard; RSS watchdog + `--max-mem`; BLOCKED (macOS FFI is unsafe)
- NBA-GROWTH — nothing bounds the pending NBAs or forked processes one time step can pile up; F4027 (`max_body_steps`) bounds steps, not memory, so a plain `always` with a zero-delay pass holding a non-blocking assignment grows to GB before it fires (PRE, under a 1.5 GB watchdog: `always begin if (en) @(posedge clk) q <= d; q2 <= d; end` with `en` 0, 1.53 GB in 0.69 s; four NBAs, 1.69 GB in 0.56 s; `begin repeat (cnt) @(posedge clk); q2 <= d; end` with `cnt` 0, 1.57 GB in 1.20 s; `always begin fork @(posedge clk) q <= d; join_none end`, 1.51 GB in 1.45 s; under a 6 GB cap the first fires F4027 at 4.7 GB and the four-NBA one does not before 6 GB; the §4.5.597 review's u1–u3 twins); `sched/propagate.rs::schedule_nba`, `native/kernel.rs` (`nba`), the fork spawn; F4027 or a sibling fatal also bounds pending NBAs and live processes per step; no oracle needed (a guard); OPEN
- EXEC-ROWS — `native::run::executor_rows` rescans per `simulate`; HELD (cache if it matters)
- DELAY-CLAMP — `#delay` above u32::MAX is clamped (frozen u32); bump or announce; OPEN
- KPRED-3RD — tier-3 lacks a "kernel can run it" layer; HELD (with dispatch)
- QUIESCE-NBA — tier-3 quiescence ignores `delayed_nba`; BLOCKED (S1d-4c-2)
- BYTE-GATE-6 — six known differences meet the S1d-4d byte gate; decide each pin; OPEN
- MON-RENDER — tier-3 refuses `$monitor` / `$strobe` (`flush_postponed`); BLOCKED (S1d-4c)
- FD-EOF + FEOF — `fd_eof` X-poison hole; `$feof` over-marked (`sysfunc_is_stmt_effect`); OPEN
- NETSLOT-PREV — `NetSlot.prev` is written but never read; OPEN
- ELAB-PHASE-BLIND — the corpus cannot see front-end cost; add a front-end-bound row; OPEN
- LOW-ROI — FMT-CACHE b, GEN-3X-STR a, QUEUE-MID-ON; HELD
- T0-SERIAL — the engine scheduler follows each serial t0 comb pass (§4.5.584) with a propagate that scans every waiter: comb5000_settle interp +9.7% (vm shares the scheduler, unmeasured; native +0.8%, ibex +0.7%); `sched/run_loop.rs`, `sched/propagate.rs`; HELD (native, the default, is flat; ER §10.2 keeps the reference interpreter out of performance work)
- T0-HOLD-CHAIN — §2 🆕 AB (a): the t0 release re-runs a released held uncertified assign in every pass of every later wave, so a held chain of N is O(N²) (§4.5.590, native, PRE vs POST: r_chain, a `$random` callee, 1k/2k/4k/8k 15.3/38.9/91.4/198.4×; hu_urandom 1k/2k/4k 14.4/35.9/81.0×; a chain of certified held assigns stays ~1.0×); `sched/t0_hold.rs` (`held_always`, the wave lanes); fix = 🆕 AB's own-wave rule (round 4's build: 0.96–1.00× on both); BLOCKED (🆕 AB (d))
- CORPUS-FETCH-GUARD — `corpus-runner fetch` (`fetch.rs:168` `clear_stale`, §4.5.603) judges a clone stale from a file parse of `.git/HEAD`: a reftable clone (`refs/heads/.invalid`), a `ref:` chain past one level and a worktree's gitfile read stale, so a clean clone at the pin is removed and re-cloned (fix: when the parse fails, `.git` is a file or `.git/reftable` exists, ask `git --git-dir <d>/.git rev-parse --verify --quiet HEAD^{commit}`); a stale clone at a local, unpushed commit with a clean tree is deleted (refuse when `git --git-dir <d>/.git rev-list -n 1 HEAD --branches --not --remotes` prints); a removal should rename the clone aside first (`src.removing-<pid>`), re-check and rename back on refusal; an untracked `.DS_Store` makes a stale clone unremovable (exit 1); bundle 1's review round 3 (R3-N1–N4); OPEN
- STORE-STACK-POS — the store path's cost depends on where its frames sit on the stack: BASE's code (9483559e, 0.00 % more instructions) with every frame below `simulate` 48–96 B lower runs `real_store` / `int_store` +4–6 % cycles, and 112–160 B lower within ±1 % (a 128-B period: 4 of 8 placements slow), while picorv32 runs 6.5–7.3 % fewer cycles at 48–112 B lower (`k_schedule_nba_scalar`); ENG-1c r3's two new `Scheduler` fields (+48 B in `simulate`'s frame) measured `real_store` +5.7–5.9 %, `int_store` +5.1–5.4 %, picorv32 -6.7–7.6 % wall vs BASE (both orders), and the landing (§4.5.628) keeps BASE's `Scheduler` size instead (W/eng/eng1c/r4 REPLAN.txt, P1_MATRIX.txt); suspected cause, unmeasured: `NetArena::write_lvalue_inner` reloads the caller's 72-B `Value` temporary with 32-B loads whose store straddles a cache line at the slow placements; acceptance: a flat `base_s` sweep — BASE with a 0–160 B frame between `simulate` and `native::run::run`, every shift within ±3 % cycles on `real_store`, `int_store` and picorv32 (W/eng/eng1c/r4/bin/mkcand.py, run_p1.sh); OPEN

### 5.c Current state

Default backend `native` (tier 3); product `--no-default-features` = one executor; the corpus runs blocking in CI on 3 OSes; codegen off.

## 5.2 Queue (start order)

Canonical start order; LOOPROMPT's NEXT mirrors it and this table wins.
One iteration takes one row, or one bundle of up to three rows whose vita source diffs are file-disjoint and whose lane tables do not meet (ER §3.7), plus at most one review-free hygiene item; a row whose files are disjoint from the rows under way may start before its slot, but a stage row's implementation starts only after the previous stage row lands. A row is a single root or one funnel's roots (an oracle, a corpus row's pinned oracle counting; outside walls and oracle splits). A row that needs a format bump takes it.
Same-root rows are one slice, and their order is a measurement (a value lane lands before a guard over an unlimited fold is deleted).

Real-design first: every defect row has a corpus witness except rows 1–8, which an external report or its fix path reproduced. A slice that moves a corpus page re-pins that row's refusal in the same commit (`DRIFTED` otherwise) and reports the page before and after.
Incoming reports pre-empt the queue; reproduce every item at HEAD first.

Real-design program (owner, 2026-10-07; replaces the structural hold of 2026-10-05; re-scoped 2026-10-15): row 9's census ran OpenTitan (19 IPs, a3490b42), VeeR EL2 / EH1 and alexforencich verilog-axis / -pcie / -uart / -i2c and judged 50 roots on 28 blocked pages — structural 12 (T a declared type re-derived per consumer 6, B name binding during lowering 2, V the constant-value domain 4), local 10, other 28 — and every root is a row below or a deferred row in §7 except C-R01 (a time-0 `always @*` wake whose two values IEEE §4.7 and §9.2.2.2.2 both allow; §2-N-3). Scope (owner, 2026-10-15): (A) the structural stages T, B and V (row 69, which reads row 66's record, inside T; V1 and V3, rows 51 and 52, whose only witness is EL2, inside V) with the held rows 1–8 merging into a stage where they meet it, and the rows under way, 22 and 73 (row 67 landed: §4.5.610, §4.5.614; row 25: §4.5.615); (B) the local and other roots of EH1 and OpenTitan with their corpus rows: EH1's rows 20, 22 and 25–30 and its corpus row 31, OpenTitan's rows 33–35, 37, 38 and 40 and its corpus rows 47 and 53; deferred to §7, each with its re-entry trigger: the EL2-only rows 54–60, EL2's corpus row 64, DPI-C's rows 61 and 62 (§3.a ⑮, not a non-goal) and verilog-pcie's corpus row 63. The program is complete when every row in scopes A and B has landed or is explicitly parked into a stage that is itself in scope. Order: C-R02's seven alexforencich configurations and the EH1 set right after the rows under way; rows 65 and 24 (the T stage's EH1-path rows) before EH1's corpus row; the OpenTitan stack's local rows; the stages T, B, V, ordered by pages opened, not by the revert ranking (V then B opens 0 of the 20 structural pages, T then B opens 12) — T's census and design are delivered (row 18, closed), V starts with its no-code census and design row (row 48); row 23 ships inside T as one slice with row 68 and row 39 inside B after row 46 (each parked: its build routed new values onto the stage's root class). A page that runs becomes a corpus row. Held rows 1–8 stay held except where a stage's fix path meets one: row 2 ships with row 45; a held row that a stage's exit re-census finds on an opened page joins that stage. For this program a blocking finding is fixed, never reverted for its round count (ER §3.6's two-round revert is suspended; from the second blocking round on one axis — the same root class — the axis is re-planned by a fresh planner, not patched; each round is a fresh agent, and a delta round re-runs only the cells its delta reaches, the full re-run once in the final round), and every round is reported. The tier letter after the rank is the review tier (ER §3.7).

The `rank` column is a row's defect class — ① a silent-wrong, ② loud→supported (§1) — not its place in the queue: the `slot` column is the start order, and the row number is a stable name.
Rows 1–5, what the external report's fix path found (§4.5.585, §4.5.587, §4.5.589), go before the ① rows 6–7 because the owner's order of 2026-10-02 keeps the report's items first. §2 🆕 AD's measured half landed (§4.5.589), and the four prerequisites that half left follow in ER §10.2's risk order: routing into an existing mechanism (rows 1–2) before new infrastructure (row 3 a per-scope constant table, row 4 an AST change and a format bump). §4.5.587 reverted §3.b `unique-const-fn` (now BLOCKED): folding the `unique` form like its plain-`if` twin inherits the twin's silent-wrongs; §4.5.589 closed the package-routine half of that prefix binding and rows 1–4 close the rest (§2 re-entry: that row's fix path). The 4-state half is a rule, not a row: §4.5.588 measured that the interpreter's 0 for a never-assigned 4-state variable cannot be fixed yet (§2 🆕 AE, BLOCKED), so every arm or lane a later row opens from loud to a value declines a run that reads one; row 5 widens the interpreter that way and waits on rows 1–4; `unique-const-fn` is re-derived from its row once rows 1–4 land (ER §3.6). `unique-const-fn`, §3.b `unique-pkg-closure` and §2 🆕 AB's residue (§4.5.590 closed its t0 run on x; the residue is BLOCKED) block §3.b `unique-if-chain`'s subroutine-body residue. Rows 6–7 are §4.5.581's prerequisites: none has a corpus witness and each redesigns shared code; row 6 ships 🆕 W with 🆕 S (a)'s i64 half (each alone descends), and both meet §2 🆕 AI (§4.5.593), which waits on 🆕 AE (BLOCKED, no row; §4.5.594 reverted it whole), so row 6 waits on it; two more of §4.5.581's wait on 🆕 AE: §2 🆕 U's wide→narrow half (§4.5.591 closed its import half) and §2 🆕 V (§4.5.592 reverted it; it also waits on the §2 "Scoping" later-walk reads line), so row 7's 🆕 T waits on both. Row 8 (§4.5.588) unblocks only 🆕 AE's `repeat` cut, and no report item waits on it, so it follows rows 6–7.

| Q-DYNPAT | queued | a dynamic-array self pattern `d = '{d[1], d[0]}` assigns element by element with no snapshot (`int`: vita and iverilog `0 0`, verilator `2 1`); the right-hand side is evaluated before the assignment; fix: the queue pattern's snapshot on the dynamic-array path | ENG-1 r2 (Q04, Q04i) | ① |
| Q-CONVT0 | queued | a continuous copy that converts between `real` and an integral type (`real c; assign c = lv;`, `assign w = r;`) takes a time-0 event where iverilog has none, and a same-delta read sees the old value (vita = verilator, iverilog the new value); input to ENG-1b's time-0 rule; fix: a destination / root kind check inside `sim-engine/src/alias.rs` `copy_alias` before such a driver joins the copy nets; size S | ENG-1 r2 (C09 C14 C15 C16) | ① |
| Q-NETDLY | queued | a net delay on a declaration without an assignment (`wire [7:0] #(2,5) w; assign w = a;`) is ignored silently, int too: vita `W t=0 \| t=10 \| t=20`, verilator `t=2 \| t=15 \| t=22`, iverilog `sorry: Net delays not supported` (loud); IEEE 1800 §10.3.3; refuse by name until lifted, or implement; census first; a release of such a net must stay right (Q-RELDELAY E08: `wire [3:0] #3 y; assign y = a;` forced at 5, released at 7: vita = verilator `S t=8 y=0001`; Q-RELDELAY restores only a delayed continuous assignment's output, so a net delay that becomes real needs the same restore; it holds at t=8 only: L09, `a` changed while forced and released at 8, vita `S t=8imm y=0010`, verilator `0001`; L08, a `#2` net delay beside `assign #3`, vita `0011` at t=12, verilator `0001`: the delay sum is ignored) | ENG-1 r2 (F2e2 real right-hand side, F2e4 int); Q-RELDELAY r1 lens-diff F7 (L08 L09) | ① |
| Q-FORCEDYN | queued | a `force` whose expression reads a dynamic-array element (`force y = d[1] * 1.0`) never re-evaluates when the element changes: vita `S t=6 y=0000… \| S t=9 y=0000…` after `d[1] = 2.0`, verilator `W t=8 y=4000…`, iverilog `sorry … evaluated once` (loud); silent wrong value, PRE = POST; IEEE 1800 §10.6.2; refuse by name or re-seed the force from the heap change | ENG-1a lens-diff r3 (K01_force_dyn_elem) | ① |
| Q-WAITDYN | queued | `wait (d[i] …)` on a dynamic-array element that no continuous reader observes is never re-checked, even for a real value change (`-1.0`): vita `A end`, iverilog and verilator `WT t=5`; PRE = POST; IEEE 1800 §9.4.3; fix: a heap change re-checks every pending `wait` over that handle | ENG-1a lens-diff r3 (G03b G03c G03d, W_none_*, W_monsame_*) | ① |

| Q-RELIMM | queued | after `release` of a net or variable with an UNDELAYED continuous driver, a read in the same process, after nothing, or by another process of the same step sees the forced value until the next settle, against IEEE 1800-2017 §10.6.2 ("immediately"): `assign y = a; force y = 4'h2; release y; $display(y)` vita `0010`, iverilog = sv2v = verilator `0001` (E42 net, E45 variable, E50 other process, R12 R13 multi-driver, R15 task, E51 / R03 R04 R06–R08 the plain bits beside a delayed part-select, and a net driven through a port, whose connection is an undelayed driver: L05 a child's `assign #3 o = i;` drives the parent's `p`, vita `S t=7imm p=0010`, all three `0001`; Q05 a forced child input port `i=0010`, all three `0001`; N10 `w[7:4]` driven through a port beside a delayed `w[3:0]`, vita `w=21`, all three `51`); evaluating the drivers at the release (Q-RELDELAY review sketches K2 / K2') calls an effectful callee the oracles never call — at t=0 before the time-0 hold's release (R29 `F t=0 x=0001`) and twice per release (R30) — and moves `--obs-procs` assign evals (R12 R13 R26 13 → 12); design: keep each undelayed driver's last computed value while its target is forced (the settle computes it, the funnel drops the write) and write that at the release, no evaluation; also a concatenation target whose other bits have an undelayed driver (the release's restore writes only the delayed driver's bits): B04 `S t=7imm y=1010` (iverilog `1100`), B05 `x01x` (`x10x`), B23 `tri` `1000` (`1001`), B27 and B45 `11xx` (`01xx`), B39 `50` (`53`), B46 `00xx` (`10xx`), D04 `wor` `1000` and D05 `wand` `1011` (`1001`), D09 d09sp `S t=7imm y=100001` (`101101`: the undelayed `assign y[3:2] = 2'b11;` bits read forced); the next event and the next time step equal iverilog's on each; pre-existing | Q-RELDELAY design review Q3 (K2 K2p); r1 lens-diff F4 (L05 Q05 N10); r2 lens-diff N2 (B04 B05 B23 B27 B45 B39 B46 D04 D05); r3 Q-MDIDXDOWN (D09 d09sp) | ① |

Measured (vita silent where iverilog = sv2v print x at the shared bit, unless noted): a multiply, K13 `for (genvar i = 0; i < 2; i++) assign y[2*i+1 -: 2] = a[2*i +: 2];` + `assign y[1] = b;` vita `S t=1 y=1101`, iverilog = sv2v `11x1`; p01 `y[2*i+3 -: 2]` + `y[2]` vita `S t=1 y=1000zz \| S t=2 y=0111zz`, iverilog = sv2v `100xzz \| 011xzz`; p09 `y[3*1 -: 2]` + `y[2]` vita `S t=1 y=zz10zz \| S t=2 y=zz11zz`, iverilog = sv2v `zz1xzz \| zz1xzz`; a unary minus, K07 `wire [0:3] y; assign y[-1 +: 2] = a; assign y[0] = b;` vita `S t=1 y=0zzz`, iverilog = sv2v `xzzz`; m06 `wire [-3:2] y; y[-1 +: 2]` + `y[0]` vita `S t=1 y=zz10zz`, iverilog = sv2v `zz1xzz`; ## 6. G2 — the AI-agent observability track

SPEC = [preview/19-ai-agent-observability.md](preview/19-ai-agent-observability.md). Teeth = a 3-way internal differential (JSONL ≡ VCD ≡ `$display`), a determinism golden, and an asymmetric mutation for anything that reports. A wrong log ranks with a silent-wrong. Ranks third, behind §2 and §3.

| stage | deliverable | size |
|---|---|---|
| OBS-2 residue | `sva.jsonl` (R-L6) · per-element array probe · real / class / event probe | M |
| OBS-1 residue | staged obs · compile-fail manifest · `--seed` · `run_id` · `-G` in `run.json` · `-D` in `source.blake3` · `results.jsonl` v2 · R-L2 `fail/*.json` · R-L5 SVA / cover counts | S-M |
| R-L4 | log-channel separation | M |
